Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The waves variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 50 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 890 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_50(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 50. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^890(S, F) is trivial. QED.
